   313	// Discover a change's delta files. → { files: [{abs, suffix}], errors: [] } — errors are
   314	// VALIDATION class (exit 2 on both surfaces): bad name, missing dir, escape, zero files.
   315	function discoverDeltas(changesDirAbs, name) {
   316	  const errors = [];
   317	  const vName = require('./resolve').validateChangeName(name);
   318	  if (!vName.ok) return { files: [], errors: [`invalid change name '${name}' (${vName.kind}) — bare kebab-case, not date-prefixed, not a reserved name`] };
   319	  const changeDir = path.join(changesDirAbs, name);
   320	  if (!fs.existsSync(changeDir)) return { files: [], errors: [`change '${name}' not found under ${changesDirAbs}`] };
   321	  if (!containsReal(changesDirAbs, changeDir)) return { files: [], errors: [`change path escapes ${changesDirAbs}: ${changeDir}`] };
   322	  const specsDir = path.join(changeDir, 'specs');
   323	  // a symlinked specs/ pointing outside would smuggle its target in as the containment ROOT —
   324	  // require specs/ itself to really live inside the change dir before trusting it as a root
   325	  if (fs.existsSync(specsDir) && !containsReal(changeDir, specsDir))
   326	    return { files: [], errors: [`specs dir escapes the change dir (symlink?): ${specsDir}`] };
   327	  const all = mdFilesUnder(specsDir);
   328	  if (!all.length) return { files: [], errors: [`no delta spec files (*.md) found under ${specsDir}`] };
   329	  const files = [];
   330	  for (const abs of all) {
   331	    if (!containsReal(specsDir, abs)) { errors.push(`delta path escapes the change root: ${abs}`); continue; }
   332	    files.push({ abs, suffix: path.relative(specsDir, abs).split(path.sep).join('/') });
   333	  }
   334	  return { files, errors };
   335	}
   336	
   337	// Build the projection: apply every delta to its mapped store file IN MEMORY.
   338	// → { texts: Map<suffix, projectedText>, modules, perModule, conflicts, casMismatches,
   339	//     hygiene, validation } — texts covers ALL store files (pass-through + projected).
   340	function buildProjection(storeRoot, deltaFiles, change) {
   341	  const texts = new Map(), perModule = new Map(), modules = [];
   342	  const conflicts = [], casMismatches = [], hygiene = [], validation = [];
   343	  const unstampedMutations = [], notes = [], repaired = [];
   344	  // per-suffix operation buckets captured at THIS parse — the one delta snapshot
   345	  // (change-scope provenance must never come from a second read of the file)
   346	  const deltaOps = new Map();
   347	  // per-suffix raw old/new block text pairs for every MODIFIED operation (rename-then-modify
   348	  // baselines the COMPLETE pre-rename block) — the integrity engine's single-snapshot input
   349	  const modifiedBlocks = new Map();
   350	  const captureModified = (suffix, storeText, delta) => {
   351	    if (!delta.MODIFIED.size) return;
   352	    const storeBlocks = parseRequirementsStrict(storeText).map;
   353	    const renamedTo = new Map(delta.RENAMED.map(([o, n]) => [n, o]));
   354	    const pairs = [];
   355	    for (const [name, newBlock] of delta.MODIFIED) {
   356	      const oldName = renamedTo.get(name) || name;
   357	      const oldBlock = storeBlocks.get(oldName);
   358	      if (oldBlock === undefined) continue;               // missing target → the merge conflict path reports it
   359	      pairs.push({ name, oldBlock, newBlock });
   360	    }
   361	    if (pairs.length) modifiedBlocks.set(suffix, pairs);
   362	  };
   363	  for (const abs of mdFilesUnder(storeRoot))
   364	    texts.set(path.relative(storeRoot, abs).split(path.sep).join('/'), fs.readFileSync(abs, 'utf8'));
   365	  for (const { abs, suffix } of deltaFiles) {
   366	    modules.push(suffix);
   367	    const target = path.join(storeRoot, ...suffix.split('/'));
   368	    if (!containsReal(storeRoot, target)) { validation.push(`store target escapes ${storeRoot}: ${target}`); continue; }
   369	    const raw = fs.readFileSync(abs, 'utf8');
   370	    if (!raw.trim()) { hygiene.push(`empty delta file: ${abs}`); continue; }
   371	    const { delta, stamp, problems } = parseDeltaStrict(raw);
   372	    if (problems.length) { hygiene.push(...problems.map((p) => `${abs}: ${p}`)); continue; }
   373	    if (deltaOpCount(delta) === 0) { hygiene.push(`parsed 0 delta operations from ${abs} — it has content but no recognized operations`); continue; }
   374	    deltaOps.set(suffix, Object.freeze({
   375	      added: [...delta.ADDED.keys()], modified: [...delta.MODIFIED.keys()],
   376	      renamedPairs: delta.RENAMED.map((p) => [...p]), removed: [...delta.REMOVED.keys()],
   377	    }));
   378	    if (!stamp && mutationOpCount(delta) > 0) unstampedMutations.push(suffix);   // WARN grade (callers print)
   379	    const storeText = texts.get(suffix) || '';
   380	    if (stamp) {
   381	      const actual = texts.has(suffix) ? fingerprint(storeText) : 'new';
   382	      if (stamp !== actual) {
   383	        // rerun repair: a mismatch whose every op is already applied is the resumed-rerun
   384	        // signature — divergence with ANY real pending op still fails exactly as before
   385	        const probe = merge(storeText, delta, change);
   386	        const pending = probe.merged.length + probe.modified.length + probe.deprecated.length + probe.renamed.length;
   387	        if (pending === 0 && probe.conflicts.length === 0) {
   388	          notes.push(`${suffix}: stamp mismatch but the delta is already fully applied — rerun accepted`);
   389	          perModule.set(suffix, probe);
   390	          repaired.push(suffix);
   391	          captureModified(suffix, storeText, delta);         // a repaired module still owes its pairs
   392	          continue;                                          // contributes no writes
   393	        }
   394	        casMismatches.push(`base mismatch for ${target}: delta expects ${stamp}, store is ${actual}`); continue;
   395	      }
   396	    }
   397	    const r = merge(storeText, delta, change);
   398	    perModule.set(suffix, r);
   399	    captureModified(suffix, storeText, delta);
   400	    if (r.conflicts.length) { conflicts.push(...r.conflicts.map((c) => `${suffix}: ${c}`)); continue; }
   401	    texts.set(suffix, renderStore(storeText, r.store));
   402	  }
   403	  modules.sort();
   404	  return { texts, modules, perModule, deltaOps, modifiedBlocks, conflicts, casMismatches, hygiene, validation, unstampedMutations, notes, repaired };
   405	}
   406	
   407	
   408	// ---- modified-block integrity engine (pure; zero fs, zero matcher) ---------------------
   409	// Compares a MODIFIED operation's old store block against its replacement and reports
   410	// structural fidelity. INFORMATIVE by contract: callers never let it change a verdict,
   411	// an exit code or write semantics.
   412	
   413	const norml = (l) => l.replace(/\s+$/, '').replace(/^\s+/, '');
   414	
   415	// closed-fence spans exactly like stripFences (an unclosed opener is ordinary text)
   416	function closedFenceSpans(text) {
   417	  const spans = [];
   418	  const re = /```[\s\S]*?```/g;
   419	  let m;
   420	  while ((m = re.exec(text)) !== null) spans.push([m.index, m.index + m[0].length]);
   421	  return spans;
   422	}
   423	
   424	// block text → { prose: [lines], scenarios: [{title, body: [lines]}] } — the requirement
   425	// heading line never participates; scenario delimiters follow state A's regex, fence-aware
   426	function scanBlockStructure(text) {
   427	  const clean = text.replace(/\r\n/g, '\n').replace(/\r/g, '\n');
   428	  const spans = closedFenceSpans(clean);
   429	  const inSpan = (idx) => spans.some(([a, b]) => idx >= a && idx < b);
   430	  const lines = clean.split('\n');
   431	  // byte offsets per line for fence-span membership
   432	  const offsets = [];
   433	  let off = 0;
   434	  for (const l of lines) { offsets.push(off); off += l.length + 1; }
   435	  const prose = [], scenarios = [];
   436	  let cur = null;                       // null = in prose; else current scenario
   437	  const headRe = /^####\s+Scenario:\s+(.*)$/;
   438	  lines.forEach((line, i) => {
   439	    if (i === 0 && /^###\s+Requirement:/.test(line)) return;   // heading never compared
   440	    const hm = headRe.exec(line);
   441	    if (hm && !inSpan(offsets[i])) { cur = { title: hm[1], body: [] }; scenarios.push(cur); return; }
   442	    const n = norml(line);
   443	    if (!n) return;
   444	    if (cur) cur.body.push(n); else prose.push(n);
   445	  });
   446	  return { prose, scenarios };
   447	}
   448	
   449	// order-preserving greedy subsequence: which OLD lines are missing from NEW
   450	function missingFrom(oldLines, newLines) {
   451	  const missing = [];
   452	  let j = 0;
   453	  for (const o of oldLines) {
   454	    let found = -1;
   455	    for (let k = j; k < newLines.length; k++) if (newLines[k] === o) { found = k; break; }
   456	    if (found === -1) missing.push(o);
   457	    else j = found + 1;
   458	  }
   459	  return missing;
   460	}
   461	
   462	// → { retained, titleChanged, dropped, added, ambiguous, missingLines } (req B4 shapes/order)
   463	function compareModifiedBlock(oldBlock, newBlock, idOf) {
   464	  const empty = { retained: [], titleChanged: [], dropped: [], added: [], ambiguous: [], missingLines: [] };
   465	  if (oldBlock.trim() === newBlock.trim()) return { ...empty };   // idempotent fast path — no scan, no idOf
   466	  const o = scanBlockStructure(oldBlock);
   467	  const n = scanBlockStructure(newBlock);
   468	  const keyOf = (title) => { const id = idOf(title); return id !== null ? { key: id, id } : { key: norml(title), id: null }; };
   469	  const group = (list) => {
   470	    const m = new Map();
   471	    list.forEach((sc, idx) => {
   472	      const { key, id } = keyOf(sc.title);
   473	      if (!m.has(key)) m.set(key, { id, occ: [] });
   474	      m.get(key).occ.push({ ...sc, idx });
   475	    });
   476	    return m;
   477	  };
   478	  const om = group(o.scenarios), nm = group(n.scenarios);
   479	  const res = { retained: [], titleChanged: [], dropped: [], added: [], ambiguous: [], missingLines: [] };
   480	  // requirement prose first
   481	  for (const line of missingFrom(o.prose, n.prose)) res.missingLines.push({ scenario: null, line });
   482	  const ambON = [], ambNewOnly = [];
   483	  for (const [key, og] of om) {
   484	    const ng = nm.get(key);
   485	    const oc = og.occ.length, nc = ng ? ng.occ.length : 0;
   486	    if (oc === 1 && nc === 1) {
   487	      const oldSc = og.occ[0], newSc = ng.occ[0];
   488	      const entry = { id: og.id, title: newSc.title };
   489	      if (norml(oldSc.title) === norml(newSc.title)) res.retained.push(entry);
   490	      else res.titleChanged.push({ id: og.id, oldTitle: oldSc.title, newTitle: newSc.title });
   491	      const scenLabel = og.id !== null ? og.id : oldSc.title;
   492	      for (const line of missingFrom(oldSc.body, newSc.body)) res.missingLines.push({ scenario: scenLabel, line });
   493	    } else if (oc >= 1 && nc === 0) {
   494	      if (oc === 1) res.dropped.push({ id: og.id, title: og.occ[0].title });
   495	      else ambON.push({ key, side: 'old', oldCount: oc, newCount: 0, idx: og.occ[0].idx });
   496	    } else {
   497	      // any side > 1 with the other >= 1
   498	      const side = oc > 1 && nc > 1 ? 'both' : (oc > 1 ? 'old' : 'new');
   499	      ambON.push({ key, side, oldCount: oc, newCount: nc, idx: og.occ[0].idx });
   500	    }
   501	  }
   502	  for (const [key, ng] of nm) {
   503	    if (om.has(key)) continue;
   504	    const nc = ng.occ.length;
   505	    if (nc === 1) res.added.push({ id: ng.id, title: ng.occ[0].title });
   506	    else ambNewOnly.push({ key, side: 'new', oldCount: 0, newCount: nc, idx: ng.occ[0].idx });
   507	  }
   508	  ambON.sort((a, b) => a.idx - b.idx);
   509	  ambNewOnly.sort((a, b) => a.idx - b.idx);
   510	  res.ambiguous = [...ambON, ...ambNewOnly].map(({ key, side, oldCount, newCount }) => ({ key, side, oldCount, newCount }));
   511	  return res;
   512	}
   513	
   514	// safe rendering: C0/DEL control characters become the middle dot BEFORE the 119+ellipsis cut
   515	function sanField(s) {
   516	  const clean = String(s).replace(/[\x00-\x08\x0b-\x1f\x7f]/g, '·').replace(/[\n\t]/g, '·');
   517	  return clean.length > 120 ? clean.slice(0, 119) + '…' : clean;
   518	}
   519	
   520	// human section shared by verify and archive; '' when every entry's four classes are empty
   521	function formatIntegrityHuman(entries) {
   522	  const printable = entries.filter((e) =>
   523	    e.dropped.length || e.missingLines.length || e.ambiguous.length || e.titleChanged.length);
   524	  if (!printable.length) return '';
   525	  const lines = ['— MODIFIED INTEGRITY —'];
   526	  for (const e of printable) {
   527	    lines.push(`  ${sanField(e.file)} · ${sanField(e.name)}: retained ${e.retained.length}, added ${e.added.length}`);
   528	    for (const t of e.titleChanged) lines.push(`    titleChanged: ${sanField(t.oldTitle)} -> ${sanField(t.newTitle)}`);
   529	    for (const d of e.dropped) lines.push(`    ! dropped: ${sanField(d.title)}`);
   530	    for (const a of e.ambiguous) lines.push(`    ! ambiguous: ${sanField(a.key)} (${a.side}; old ${a.oldCount}/new ${a.newCount})`);
   531	    for (const ml of e.missingLines) lines.push(`    ! missing (${ml.scenario === null ? 'prose' : sanField(ml.scenario)}): ${sanField(ml.line)}`);
   532	  }
   533	  return lines.join('\n');
   534	}
   535	
   536	
   537	// The archive-side integrity section. The matcher factory is INJECTED at the bin seam
   538	// ((cwd) => matcher | {error}) so this module never requires spec-runner; a missing factory
   539	// degrades exactly like a factory error — one bounded stderr warning, report skipped,
   540	// every other archive output/exit/write unchanged.
   541	function pushIntegritySection(out, err, modifiedBlocks, idMatcherFactory, cwd) {
   542	  if (!modifiedBlocks || modifiedBlocks.size === 0) return;
   543	  const sanitizeMsg = require('./config').sanitizeMsg;
   544	  const warn = (reason) => err.push(sanitizeMsg('warning: modified-integrity ' + reason));
   545	  if (!idMatcherFactory) { warn('id matcher unavailable (no factory injected)'); return; }
   546	  let matcher;
   547	  try { matcher = idMatcherFactory(cwd); } catch (e) { warn('id-pattern resolution failed: ' + String(e.message || e)); return; }
   548	  if (!matcher || matcher.error) { warn('id-pattern resolution failed: ' + String(matcher && matcher.error)); return; }
   549	  // one batch over every old+new block scenario title
   550	  const titles = [];
   551	  const collect = (text) => {
   552	    const clean = text.replace(/\r\n/g, '\n').replace(/\r/g, '\n');
   553	    const spans = closedFenceSpans(clean);
   554	    const inSpan = (idx) => spans.some(([a, b]) => idx >= a && idx < b);
   555	    let off = 0;
   556	    for (const line of clean.split('\n')) {
   557	      const m = /^####\s+Scenario:\s+(.*)$/.exec(line);
   558	      if (m && !inSpan(off)) titles.push(m[1]);
   559	      off += line.length + 1;
   560	    }
   561	  };
   562	  for (const list of modifiedBlocks.values()) for (const { oldBlock, newBlock } of list) { collect(oldBlock); collect(newBlock); }
   563	  const res = matcher.batch(titles);
   564	  if (res.failure) { warn('matcher failure: ' + res.failure); return; }
   565	  const titleId = new Map();
   566	  titles.forEach((t, i) => { if (!titleId.has(t)) titleId.set(t, res.ids[i]); });
   567	  const idOf = (t) => { const v = titleId.get(t); return v === undefined ? null : v; };
   568	  const entries = [];
   569	  for (const [file, list] of modifiedBlocks)
   570	    for (const { name, oldBlock, newBlock } of list)
   571	      entries.push({ file, name, ...compareModifiedBlock(oldBlock, newBlock, idOf) });
   572	  entries.sort((a, b) => (a.file === b.file ? (a.name < b.name ? -1 : 1) : (a.file < b.file ? -1 : 1)));
   573	  const seg = formatIntegrityHuman(entries);
   574	  if (seg) out.push('\n' + seg);
   575	}
   576	
   577	// ---- archive commit machinery ----------------------------------------------
   578	
   579	// colon-free date-time stamp YYYY-MM-DDThhmm (CLI's own clock; never an agent guess)
   580	function archiveStamp(d) {
   581	  const p = (n) => String(n).padStart(2, '0');
   582	  return `${d.getFullYear()}-${p(d.getMonth() + 1)}-${p(d.getDate())}T${p(d.getHours())}${p(d.getMinutes())}`;
   583	}
   584	
   585	const CHANGE_NAME_RE = /^[a-z0-9]+(-[a-z0-9]+)*$/;   // bare kebab-case — same rule as `apriori new`
   586	
   587	
   588	// Move an in-flight change dir (bare name) → changes/archive/<stamp>-<name>/
   589	// Fail-closed: source AND destination must resolve (realpath) strictly inside changesDir.
   590	function archiveChangeDir(changesDir, name, now, ops) {
   590	function archiveChangeDir(changesDir, name, now, ops) {
   591	  const rename = (ops && ops.renameSync) || fs.renameSync;
   592	  if (!CHANGE_NAME_RE.test(name)) throw new Error(`invalid change name '${name}' — bare kebab-case only`);
   593	  const src = path.join(changesDir, name);
   594	  if (!fs.existsSync(src)) return null;
   595	  if (!containsReal(changesDir, src)) throw new Error(`change path escapes ${changesDir}`);
   596	  const dest = path.join(changesDir, 'archive', `${archiveStamp(now)}-${name}`);
   597	  if (!containsReal(changesDir, dest)) throw new Error(`archive destination escapes ${changesDir} (symlinked archive/?)`);
   598	  fs.mkdirSync(path.dirname(dest), { recursive: true });
   599	  rename(src, dest);
   600	  return dest;
   601	}
   602	
   603	const CATEGORY_LABELS = [['merged (ADDED)', 'merged'], ['modified (MODIFIED)', 'modified'],
   604	  ['deprecated (REMOVED)', 'deprecated'], ['renamed (RENAMED)', 'renamed'], ['already merged (no-op)', 'unchanged']];
   605	
   606	// High-level archive: the whole change, four phases (preflight → stage → commit → move).
   607	// → { code, out: [], err: [] } — pure return; cli() prints. ops is the fault-injection seam.
   608	function archiveChange(o) {
   609	  const out = [], err = [];
   610	  const ops = o.ops || { writeFileSync: fs.writeFileSync.bind(fs), renameSync: fs.renameSync.bind(fs), rmSync: fs.rmSync.bind(fs) };
   611	  const cwd = o.cwd || process.cwd();
   612	  const changesDir = path.resolve(cwd, o.changesDir || path.join('apriori', 'changes'));
   613	  const storeRoot = path.resolve(cwd, 'apriori', 'specs');
   614	
   615	  // --- phase 1: preflight (no writes) ---
   616	  const d = discoverDeltas(changesDir, o.change);
   617	  if (d.errors.length) { err.push(...d.errors.map((e) => `archive: ${e}`)); return { code: 2, out, err }; }
   618	  const p = buildProjection(storeRoot, d.files, o.change);
   619	  if (p.validation.length) { err.push(...p.validation.map((e) => `archive: ${e}`)); return { code: 2, out, err }; }
   620	  // per-module report for whatever merged cleanly (dry-run info comes first, then failures)
   621	  for (const suffix of p.modules) {
   622	    const r = p.perModule.get(suffix);
   623	    if (!r || r.conflicts.length) continue;
   624	    out.push(`--- ${suffix}`);
   625	    for (const [label, key] of CATEGORY_LABELS) if (r[key].length) out.push(`${label}: ${r[key].join(', ')}`);
   626	  }
   627	  const casDenials = [];
   628	  if (p.unstampedMutations.length) {
   629	    const w = casWaiver(cwd, o.noCas);
   630	    if (w.problem) {
   631	      casDenials.push(`${w.problem} — a broken config never equals a waiver (denied)`);
   632	    } else if (!w.waiver) {
   633	      for (const sfx of p.unstampedMutations)
   634	        casDenials.push(`${unstampedWarning(sfx)} — denied by default; waive with --no-cas or a | cas | optional | config row`);
   635	    } else {
   636	      for (const sfx of p.unstampedMutations) out.push(`warning: ${unstampedWarning(sfx)}`);
   637	      out.push(`note: cas waived by ${w.waiver}`);
   638	    }
   639	  }
   640	  for (const n of p.notes) out.push(`note: ${n}`);
   641	  // CAS denial joins the preflight failure set — every diagnosis stays visible in one report
   642	  const preflightFailures = [...casDenials, ...p.hygiene, ...p.casMismatches, ...p.conflicts];
   643	  if (preflightFailures.length) {
   644	    for (const f of preflightFailures) err.push(`archive: ${f}`);
   645	    out.push('\nRESULT: FAILED PREFLIGHT — nothing written');
   646	    return { code: 1, out, err };
   647	  }
   648	  const repaired = new Set(p.repaired);                     // already applied — no writes for these
   649	  const jobs = d.files.filter(({ suffix }) => !repaired.has(suffix)).map(({ suffix }) => {
   650	    const store = path.join(storeRoot, ...suffix.split('/'));
   651	    return { suffix, store, tmp: store + '.tmp-archive', text: p.texts.get(suffix) };
   652	  }).sort((a, b) => (a.store < b.store ? -1 : 1));
   653	  if (o.write) {
   654	    for (const j of jobs) if (fs.existsSync(j.tmp)) {
   655	      err.push(`archive: temp file already exists: ${j.tmp} — another run in flight or a manual-recovery artifact; not touching it`);
   656	      out.push('\nRESULT: FAILED PREFLIGHT — nothing written');
   657	      return { code: 1, out, err };
   658	    }
   659	    if (o.changesDirExplicit) {
   660	      // destination containment is checked before any write, not at move time
   661	      const probe = path.join(changesDir, 'archive', 'probe');
   662	      if (!containsReal(changesDir, probe)) {
   663	        err.push(`archive: archive destination escapes ${changesDir} (symlinked archive/?)`);
   664	        return { code: 2, out, err };
   665	      }
   666	    }
   667	  }
   668	  // the modified-block integrity section: AFTER every preflight guard (incl. the --write
   669	  // temp/containment checks above), BEFORE any write-result line — informative only
   670	  pushIntegritySection(out, err, p.modifiedBlocks, o.idMatcherFactory, cwd);
   671	  if (!o.write) {
   672	    out.push(`\nRESULT: MERGED (dry-run; ${jobs.length} module(s)) — pass --write to apply`);
   673	    return { code: 0, out, err };
   674	  }
   675	
   676	  // --- phase 2: stage (this run's temps only — preflight proved none pre-existed) ---
   677	  const staged = [];
   678	  try {
   679	    for (const j of jobs) {
   680	      fs.mkdirSync(path.dirname(j.store), { recursive: true });
   681	      ops.writeFileSync(j.tmp, j.text);
   682	      staged.push(j.tmp);
   683	    }
   684	  } catch (e) {
   685	    for (const t of staged) { try { ops.rmSync(t, { force: true }); } catch { /* best effort */ } }
   686	    err.push(`archive: staging failed (${e.message}) — stores untouched, this run's temp files removed`);
   687	    return { code: 1, out, err };
   688	  }
   689	
   690	  // --- phase 3: commit (per-file atomic renames, sorted order; no rollback on mid-commit failure) ---
   691	  const committed = [];
   692	  for (const j of jobs) {
   693	    try { ops.renameSync(j.tmp, j.store); committed.push(j); }
   694	    catch (e) {
   695	      err.push(`archive: COMMIT FAILED at ${j.suffix} (${e.message})`);
   696	      err.push(`  committed:     ${committed.map((c) => c.suffix).join(', ') || '(none)'}`);
   697	      const remaining = jobs.filter((x) => !committed.includes(x));
   698	      err.push(`  NOT committed: ${remaining.map((x) => x.suffix).join(', ')}`);
   699	      err.push(`  temp files remaining for manual completion: ${remaining.map((x) => x.tmp).join(', ')}`);
   700	      return { code: 1, out, err };
   701	    }
   702	  }
   703	
   704	  // --- phase 4: move (only with explicit --changes-dir) ---
   705	  let moved = null;
   706	  if (o.changesDirExplicit) {
   707	    try { moved = archiveChangeDir(changesDir, o.change, new Date(), ops); }
   708	    catch (e) { err.push(`archive: stores committed but the change-dir move failed: ${e.message} — rerun to complete`); return { code: 1, out, err }; }
   709	    if (moved === null) { err.push('archive: stores committed but the change dir vanished before the move'); return { code: 1, out, err }; }
   710	  }
   711	  out.push(`\nRESULT: MERGED — ${jobs.length} module store(s) rewritten${moved ? `; change archived → ${moved}` : ''}`);
   712	  return { code: 0, out, err };
   713	}
   714	
   715	// print the CAS stamp line for a store file's current content
   716	const STAMP_USAGE = 'usage: apriori stamp <store-file>';
   717	function stampCli(argv) {
   718	  return withStrict(argv, { sub: 'stamp', usage: STAMP_USAGE, positionals: 1, flags: {} }, (f, pos) => {
   719	    const p = pos[0];
   720	    if (!fs.existsSync(p)) { console.log('<!-- apriori-base: new -->'); return 0; }
   721	    if (fs.statSync(p).isDirectory()) { console.error(`stamp: ${p} is a directory, not a store file`); return 2; }
   722	    let text;
   723	    try { text = fs.readFileSync(p, 'utf8'); }
   724	    catch (e) { console.error(`stamp: cannot read ${p}: ${e.message}`); return 2; }
   725	    console.log(`<!-- apriori-base: ${fingerprint(text)} -->`);
   726	    return 0;
   727	  });
   728	}
   729	
   730	const USAGE = 'usage: apriori archive --store <f> --delta <f> --change <name> [--write] [--changes-dir <dir>] [--no-cas]\n   or: apriori archive --change <name> [--write] [--changes-dir <dir>] [--no-cas]';
   731	
   732	function cli(argv, deps = {}) {
   733	  return withStrict(argv, { sub: 'archive', usage: USAGE, positionals: 0,
   734	    flags: { '--store': 'value', '--delta': 'value', '--change': 'value', '--write': 'flag', '--changes-dir': 'value', '--no-cas': 'flag' } }, (f) => {
   735	    const a = { store: f['--store'] || null, delta: f['--delta'] || null, change: f['--change'] || null,
   736	      write: !!f['--write'], changesDir: f['--changes-dir'] || null, noCas: !!f['--no-cas'] };
   737	  // high-level form: --change alone (no --store/--delta) drives the whole change
   738	    if (a.change && !a.store && !a.delta) {
   739	      const r = archiveChange({ cwd: process.cwd(), change: a.change, write: a.write,
   740	        changesDir: a.changesDir || undefined, changesDirExplicit: !!a.changesDir, noCas: a.noCas,
   741	        idMatcherFactory: deps.idMatcherFactory });
   742	      for (const l of r.out) console.log(l);
   743	      for (const l of r.err) console.error(l);
   744	      return r.code;
   745	    }
   746	    if (a.change && (a.store || a.delta) && !(a.store && a.delta)) {
   747	      console.error('usage: apriori archive --change <name> [--write] [--changes-dir <dir>]  (high-level form)\n   or: apriori archive --store <f> --delta <f> --change <name> [--write] [--changes-dir <dir>]  (single-file form)\n(--change cannot be combined with only one of --store/--delta)');
   748	      return 2;
   749	    }
   750	    if (!a.store || !a.delta || !a.change) { console.error(USAGE); return 2; }
   750	    if (!a.store || !a.delta || !a.change) { console.error(USAGE); return 2; }
   751	    const vEarly = require('./resolve').validateChangeName(a.change);
   752	    if (!vEarly.ok) { console.error(`archive: invalid change name '${a.change}' (${vEarly.kind}) — bare kebab-case, not date-prefixed, not a reserved name`); return 2; }
   753	    if (a.changesDir && !fs.existsSync(path.join(a.changesDir, a.change))) {
   754	      console.error(`archive: change '${a.change}' not found under ${a.changesDir} — nothing to move`); return 2;
   755	    }
   756	    // first archive in a repo: the store file may not exist yet — start from an empty store
   757	    const storeExists = fs.existsSync(a.store);
   758	    const storeText = storeExists ? fs.readFileSync(a.store, 'utf8') : '';
   759	    if (!storeExists) console.log(`store ${a.store} does not exist yet — will be created${a.write ? '' : ' on --write'}`);
   760	    const deltaText = fs.readFileSync(a.delta, 'utf8');
   761	    const { delta, stamp, problems } = parseDeltaStrict(deltaText);
   762	    if (problems.length) {
   763	      for (const p of problems) console.error(`archive: ${a.delta}: ${p}`);
   764	      console.log('\nRESULT: MALFORMED DELTA — nothing written');
   765	      return 1;
   766	    }
   767	    // Guard against a silent no-op: a delta with real content that parsed to zero operations
   768	    // is almost always a format mismatch (wrong heading level/keyword), not an empty change.
   769	    if (deltaText.trim() && deltaOpCount(delta) === 0) {
   770	      console.error(`archive: parsed 0 delta operations from ${a.delta} — it has content but no recognized operations.`);
   771	      console.error('  expected `## ADDED|MODIFIED|REMOVED Requirements` (h2) with `### Requirement: <name>` (h3) blocks,');
   772	      console.error('  or `## RENAMED Requirements` with `- Old -> New` lines. Nothing written.');
   773	      return 1;
   774	    }
   775	    // AM-32 (4.0): mutation ops without a stamp are denied unless visibly waived
   776	    if (!stamp && mutationOpCount(delta) > 0) {
   777	      const w = casWaiver(process.cwd(), a.noCas);
   778	      if (w.problem || !w.waiver) {
   779	        console.error(`archive: ${w.problem ? w.problem + ' — a broken config never equals a waiver (denied)' : unstampedWarning(a.delta) + ' — denied by default; waive with --no-cas or a | cas | optional | config row'}`);
   780	        console.log('\nRESULT: FAILED PREFLIGHT — nothing written');
   781	        return 1;
   782	      }
   783	      console.log(`warning: ${unstampedWarning(a.delta)}`);
   784	      console.log(`note: cas waived by ${w.waiver}`);
   785	    }
   786	    // CAS: a present base stamp must match the store the delta is about to merge into (AM-24/AM-26)
   787	    if (stamp) {
   788	      const actual = storeExists ? fingerprint(storeText) : 'new';
   789	      if (stamp !== actual) {
   790	        console.error(`archive: base mismatch for ${a.store}: delta expects ${stamp}, store is ${actual} — nothing written (serialize per §4.11)`);
   791	        return 1;
   792	      }
   793	    }
   794	    const { store, merged, modified, deprecated, renamed, conflicts, unchanged } = merge(storeText, delta, a.change);
   795	
   796	    for (const [label, ids] of [['merged (ADDED)', merged], ['modified (MODIFIED)', modified], ['deprecated (REMOVED)', deprecated], ['renamed (RENAMED)', renamed], ['already merged (no-op)', unchanged]])
   797	      if (ids.length) console.log(`${label}: ${ids.join(', ')}`);
   798	
   799	    if (conflicts.length) {
   800	      console.log('\nCONFLICTS (stop — open a ledger issue, human resolves):');
   801	      for (const c of conflicts) console.log(`  ✗ ${c}`);
   802	      console.log('\nRESULT: CONFLICT — nothing written');
   803	      return 1;
   804	    }
   805	    if (a.write) {
   806	      // Transactional ordering: stage the store to a temp file, do the (atomic) dir move, then
   807	      // commit the store via rename. If the move fails, the temp is discarded and the store on
   808	      // disk stays byte-for-byte untouched — never a half-completed archive.
   809	      fs.mkdirSync(path.dirname(a.store), { recursive: true });
   810	      const tmp = a.store + '.tmp-archive';
   811	      fs.writeFileSync(tmp, renderStore(storeText, store));
   812	      let moved = null;
   813	      if (a.changesDir) {
   814	        try { moved = archiveChangeDir(a.changesDir, a.change, new Date()); }
   815	        catch (e) { fs.rmSync(tmp, { force: true }); console.error(`archive: ${e.message} — store left untouched`); return 1; }
   816	        if (moved === null) { fs.rmSync(tmp, { force: true }); console.error(`archive: change dir vanished mid-run — store left untouched`); return 1; }
   817	      }
   818	      fs.renameSync(tmp, a.store);
   819	      console.log(`\nRESULT: MERGED — ${a.store} rewritten${moved ? `; change archived → ${moved}` : ''}`);
   820	    } else {
   821	      console.log(`\nRESULT: MERGED (dry-run; ${store.size} requirements in result) — pass --write to apply`);
   822	    }
   823	    return 0;
   824	  });
   825	}
   826	module.exports = { parseRequirements, parseRequirementsStrict, parseDelta, parseDeltaStrict, deltaOpCount, mutationOpCount, unstampedWarning,
   827	  compareModifiedBlock, formatIntegrityHuman,
   828	  merge, renderStore, archiveStamp, archiveChangeDir, archiveChange, CHANGE_NAME_RE,
   829	  fingerprint, parseStamp, containsReal, discoverDeltas, buildProjection,
   830	  DEPRECATED_RE, stripDeprecatedBlocks, stampCli, cli };
    90	  if (st.isFile()) return [target];
    91	  const out = [];
    92	  for (const e of fs.readdirSync(target, { withFileTypes: true })) {
    93	    const p = path.join(target, e.name);
    94	    if (e.isDirectory()) out.push(...mdFiles(p));
    95	    else if (e.name.endsWith('.md')) out.push(p);
    96	  }
    97	  return out.sort();
    98	}
    99	
   100	// Shared scenario scan over one text — STRUCTURE ONLY (no ID extraction; that is the
   101	// matcher's job so config-origin patterns stay inside the terminable channel).
   102	// Fenced content is documentation; scenarios inside DEPRECATED requirement blocks
   103	// (the marker REMOVED injects) are excluded in every form — they are neither demanded
   104	// nor listed, and their lingering tests become ORPHAN.
   105	function scanTitles(text, label, pairs, preClean) {
   106	  const clean = preClean ? text : am.stripDeprecatedBlocks(stripFences(text));
   107	  let m;
   108	  SCENARIO_RE.lastIndex = 0;
   109	  while ((m = SCENARIO_RE.exec(clean)) !== null) pairs.push([label, m[1]]);
   110	}
   111	
   112	// One batch over tagged pairs ([label,title,scoped]) plus sibling titles (appended to the
   113	// SAME batch — never a third matcher call) → { acc, ids, siblingIds } or { failure }.
   114	function makeThenBind(pairs, matcher, siblingTitles = [], integrityTitles = []) {
   115	  const titles = [...pairs.map((p) => p[1]), ...siblingTitles, ...integrityTitles];
   116	  const res = matcher.batch(titles);
   117	  if (res.failure) return { failure: res.failure };
   118	  const acc = { byId: new Map(), idFiles: new Map(), unidentified: [] };
   119	  pairs.forEach(([label, title], i) => {
   120	    const id = res.ids[i];
   121	    if (id) {
   122	      if (!acc.byId.has(id)) { acc.byId.set(id, []); acc.idFiles.set(id, []); }
   123	      acc.byId.get(id).push(title);
   124	      acc.idFiles.get(id).push(label);
   125	    } else acc.unidentified.push([label, title]);
   126	  });
   127	  const sibSlice = res.ids.slice(pairs.length, pairs.length + siblingTitles.length);
   128	  const siblingIds = new Set(sibSlice.filter((x) => x !== null));
   129	  const integrityIds = res.ids.slice(pairs.length + siblingTitles.length);
   130	  return { acc, ids: res.ids.slice(0, pairs.length), siblingIds, integrityIds };
   131	}
   132	
   133	// Scenario titles a SIBLING active change genuinely declares — an EXEMPTION source, so it
   134	// is strict: each sibling delta must parse cleanly (parseDeltaStrict, zero problems, >0 ops)
   135	// and only scenarios inside its ADDED/MODIFIED block bodies count (a rename-only sibling's
   136	// scenarios already live in this change's projection and bind as out-of-scope reds).
   137	// Anomalies — malformed deltas, symlinks, escaping paths, unreadable files — grant NOTHING:
   138	// skipping is the fail-closed direction here (fewer exemptions = a stricter verdict); they
   139	// are never an ERROR, or a broken sibling could block every other change's gate.
   140	// fault-tolerant .md walk for the sibling scan ONLY: every readdir/lstat failure at any
   141	// depth yields zero entries from that branch — an anomaly must never throw out of verify()
   142	// nor create an infra ERROR (it merely grants no exemption)
   143	function safeMdWalk(dir) {
   144	  const out = [];
   145	  let entries;
   480	      lines.push(`        ${raw.length > 120 ? raw.slice(0, 119) + '…' : raw}`);
   481	    if (sr2.unattributedFailures.count > 20) lines.push(`        … and ${sr2.unattributedFailures.count - 20} more`);
   482	    lines.push(`    duplicate IDs: ${sr2.duplicates.length}${sr2.duplicates.length ? `  (${sr2.duplicates.map((d) => `${d.id}: ${d.files.map((f) => path.basename(f)).join(', ')}`).join('; ')})` : ''}`);
   483	  }
   484	  const errs = extras.errors || [];
   485	  lines.push('\nRESULT: ' + (errs.length ? 'ERROR — run not trustworthy (see stderr)'
   486	    : (v.clean && !(extras.duplicates || []).length ? 'GREEN — spec is the test suite' : 'GAPS')));
   487	  return lines.join('\n');
   488	}
   489	
   490	// Projection inputs for `verify --change` (all failure classes → run.errors, exit 2).
   491	// Roots resolve against cwd exactly as `apriori status` resolves change dirs.
   492	function buildChangeProjection(change, cwd) {
   493	  const changesDir = path.resolve(cwd, 'apriori', 'changes');
   494	  const storeRoot = path.resolve(cwd, 'apriori', 'specs');
   495	  const projection = { change, modules: [], conflicts: [], unstampedMutations: [] };
   496	  const d = am.discoverDeltas(changesDir, change);
   497	  if (d.errors.length) return { projection, errors: d.errors, texts: null };
   498	  const p = am.buildProjection(storeRoot, d.files, change);
   499	  projection.modules = p.modules;
   500	  projection.conflicts = p.conflicts;
   501	  projection.unstampedMutations = p.unstampedMutations;      // WARN grade — cli prints, verdict untouched
   502	  projection.notes = p.notes;
   503	  const errors = [...p.validation, ...p.hygiene, ...p.casMismatches];
   504	  if (p.conflicts.length) errors.push(`projection has ${p.conflicts.length} merge conflict(s) — no trustworthy projection: ${p.conflicts.join('; ')}`);
   505	  // deltaOps/modifiedBlocks travel with the SAME parse snapshot the projection used (never re-read)
   506	  return { projection, errors, texts: errors.length ? null : p.texts, deltaOps: p.deltaOps, modifiedBlocks: p.modifiedBlocks };
   507	}
   508	
   509	// ---- change scope (verify --change): requirement-block provenance over the projection ----
   510	// Collect every scenario occurrence of the projection ONCE, tagging each pair with whether
   511	// it lives inside a change-scoped block (produced/renamed by ADDED/MODIFIED/RENAMED; blocks
   512	// whose final projected state is deprecated are excluded — they are stripped before scanning).
   513	function collectChangePairs(texts, deltaOps) {
   514	  const pairs = [];                       // [label, title, scoped]
   515	  const requirements = [];                // [{file, name, operations}]
   516	  for (const [label, text] of texts) {
   517	    const clean = am.stripDeprecatedBlocks(stripFences(text));
   518	    const ops = deltaOps.get(label);
   519	    if (!ops) {
   520	      const sub = [];
   521	      scanTitles(clean, label, sub, true);
   522	      pairs.push(...sub.map(([l, t]) => [l, t, false]));
   523	      continue;
   524	    }
   525	    const removed = new Set(ops.removed);
   526	    const renamedNew = ops.renamedPairs.map(([, n]) => n);
   527	    const scopeNames = new Set([...ops.added, ...ops.modified, ...renamedNew].filter((n) => !removed.has(n)));
   528	    const { map } = am.parseRequirementsStrict(clean);
   529	    const firstBlock = clean.search(/^###\s+Requirement:/m);
   530	    const preamble = firstBlock >= 0 ? clean.slice(0, firstBlock) : clean;
   531	    const sub = [];
   532	    scanTitles(preamble, label, sub, true);
   533	    pairs.push(...sub.map(([l, t]) => [l, t, false]));
   534	    for (const [name, block] of map) {
   535	      const scoped = scopeNames.has(name);
   536	      if (scoped) {
   537	        const operations = [];
   538	        if (renamedNew.includes(name)) operations.push('RENAMED');
   539	        if (ops.added.includes(name)) operations.push('ADDED');
   540	        if (ops.modified.includes(name)) operations.push('MODIFIED');
   541	        requirements.push({ file: label, name, operations });
   542	      }
   543	      const bsub = [];
   544	      scanTitles(block, label, bsub, true);
   545	      pairs.push(...bsub.map(([l, t]) => [l, t, scoped]));
   546	    }
   547	  }
   548	  requirements.sort((a, b) => (a.file === b.file ? (a.name < b.name ? -1 : 1) : (a.file < b.file ? -1 : 1)));
   549	  return { pairs, requirements };
   550	}
   551	
   552	// per-kind op counts for the vacuous note: '(removal-only change)' or '(ops: ADDED 1, REMOVED 2)'
   553	function vacuousNoteFor(deltaOps) {
   554	  const counts = { ADDED: 0, MODIFIED: 0, RENAMED: 0, REMOVED: 0 };
   555	  for (const ops of deltaOps.values()) {
   556	    counts.ADDED += ops.added.length; counts.MODIFIED += ops.modified.length;
   557	    counts.RENAMED += ops.renamedPairs.length; counts.REMOVED += ops.removed.length;
   558	  }
   559	  const removalOnly = counts.REMOVED > 0 && !counts.ADDED && !counts.MODIFIED && !counts.RENAMED;
   560	  if (removalOnly) return '0 scenario(s) in change scope (removal-only change)';
   561	  const parts = Object.entries(counts).filter(([, n]) => n > 0).map(([k, n]) => `${k} ${n}`);
   562	  return `0 scenario(s) in change scope (ops: ${parts.join(', ')})`;
   563	}
   564	
   565	// an errors-only run shape shared by every pre-test early exit (failed projection,
   566	// invalid pattern, matcher-channel failure) — fail closed WITHOUT running any tests
   567	function errorsRun(errors, projection) {
   568	  const verdict = evaluate(new Map(), [], new Map(), []);
   569	  const run = { verdict, results: new Map(), fileCount: 0, failCount: 0, scenarioCount: 0,
   570	    missingTargets: [], duplicates: [], bailout: null, noTap: false, tapProblems: [], stderr: '',
   571	    exec: { status: null, signal: null, error: null }, errors };
   572	  if (projection) run.projection = projection;
   573	  return run;
   574	}
   575	
   576	// enumeration-only projection metadata for the invalid-pattern early exit on --change runs:
   577	// SR-23's projection contract survives, yet no delta CONTENT is read (directory listing only)
   578	function enumerateProjection(change, cwd) {
   579	  const changesDir = path.resolve(cwd, 'apriori', 'changes');
   580	  const projection = { change, modules: [], conflicts: [], unstampedMutations: [] };
   581	  const d = am.discoverDeltas(changesDir, change);
   582	  if (!d.errors.length) projection.modules = d.files.map((f) => f.suffix);
   583	  return projection;
   584	}
   585	
   586	// Full verify run. opts: { specs:[], change, testCmd, idPattern, cwd }
   587	function verify(opts) {
   588	  const cwd = opts.cwd || '.';
   589	  // pattern resolution FIRST — before any spec content is read or any test command spawned
   590	  const resolved = resolveIdPattern(cwd, opts.idPattern === undefined ? null : opts.idPattern);
   591	  if (resolved.error)
   592	    return errorsRun([resolved.error], opts.change ? enumerateProjection(opts.change, cwd) : null);
   593	  const idRe = new RegExp(resolved.source);
   594	  const matcher = makeIdMatcher(resolved);
   595	  let collected, projection = null, changeMeta = null;
   596	  if (opts.change) {
   597	    const b = buildChangeProjection(opts.change, cwd);
   598	    projection = b.projection;
   599	    if (!b.texts) return errorsRun(b.errors, projection);
   600	    // one pass over the projection: every occurrence collected once, tagged with its
   601	    // change-scope provenance; ONE matcher batch serves both views (SR-63).
   602	    // Sibling active changes' delta scenario titles join the SAME batch — a failing
   603	    // orphan whose ID a sibling declares is provably that change's business (GT-26).
   604	    const { pairs, requirements } = collectChangePairs(b.texts, b.deltaOps);
   605	    const siblingTitles = collectSiblingTitles(cwd, opts.change);
   606	    // OLD-block scenario titles ride the SAME batch (integrity report input; bind nothing)
   607	    const integrityTitles = [];
   608	    for (const list of (b.modifiedBlocks || new Map()).values())
   609	      for (const { oldBlock } of list) scanTitles(oldBlock, '(integrity)', integrityTitles);
   610	    const integrityTitleTexts = integrityTitles.map(([, t]) => t);
   611	    const batch = makeThenBind(pairs, matcher, siblingTitles, integrityTitleTexts);
   612	    if (batch.failure) return errorsRun([matcherFailureMsg(batch.failure)], projection);
   613	    collected = { byId: batch.acc.byId, unidentified: batch.acc.unidentified,
   614	      files: [...b.texts.keys()], missingTargets: [], duplicates: dupsOf(batch.acc) };
   615	    const titleId = new Map();
   616	    pairs.forEach(([, t], i) => { if (!titleId.has(t)) titleId.set(t, batch.ids[i]); });
   617	    integrityTitleTexts.forEach((t, i) => { if (!titleId.has(t)) titleId.set(t, batch.integrityIds[i]); });
   618	    changeMeta = { pairs, ids: batch.ids, requirements, deltaOps: b.deltaOps, siblingIds: batch.siblingIds,
   619	      modifiedBlocks: b.modifiedBlocks, titleId };
   620	  } else {
   621	    collected = collectScenarios(opts.specs, idRe, matcher);
   622	    if (collected.failure) return errorsRun([matcherFailureMsg(collected.failure)], projection);
   623	  }
   624	  const { byId, unidentified, files, missingTargets, duplicates } = collected;
   625	  const exec = runTestCommand(opts.testCmd, cwd);
   626	  const parsed = parseTap(exec.out, idRe, matcher);
   627	  if (parsed.matchFailure) {
   628	    const run = errorsRun([matcherFailureMsg(parsed.matchFailure)], projection);
   629	    run.exec = { status: exec.status, signal: exec.signal, error: exec.error };
   630	    run.stderr = exec.stderr || '';
   631	    return run;
   632	  }
   633	  const { results, untagged, untaggedFails, unattributedFailures, bailout, plans, points, dupNumbers, tapProblems } = parsed;
   634	  const verdict = evaluate(byId, unidentified, results, unattributedFailures);
   635	  const failCount = [...results.values()].reduce((s, r) => s + r.fail, 0) + untaggedFails;
   636	  const run = {
   637	    verdict, results, fileCount: files.length, failCount,
   638	    scenarioCount: byId.size + unidentified.length,
   639	    missingTargets, duplicates, bailout, plans, points, dupNumbers,
   640	    noTap: zeroTapParsed(exec.out, results, untagged, unattributedFailures)
   641	      || (!exec.out.trim() && looksLikeTap(exec.stderr)),  // TAP went to stderr — same zero-TAP cure (2>&1)
   642	    tapProblems,
   643	    stderr: exec.stderr || '',
   644	    exec: { status: exec.status, signal: exec.signal, error: exec.error },
   645	  };
   646	  if (projection) run.projection = projection;
   647	  run.errors = infraErrors(run);
   648	  // change-scoped verdict split (SR-56..62): only on trustworthy --change runs; every
   649	  // ERROR class leaves the run without storeReport/changeScope (absent, never null)
   650	  if (changeMeta && run.errors.length === 0) {
   651	    applyChangeScope(run, changeMeta);
   652	    // the modified-block integrity report (informative; ALWAYS present on GREEN/GAPS)
   653	    const idOf = (title) => { const v = changeMeta.titleId.get(title); return v === undefined ? null : v; };
   654	    const entries = [];
   655	    for (const [file, list] of (changeMeta.modifiedBlocks || new Map()))
   656	      for (const { name, oldBlock, newBlock } of list)
   657	        entries.push({ file, name, ...am.compareModifiedBlock(oldBlock, newBlock, idOf) });
   658	    entries.sort((a, b2) => (a.file === b2.file ? (a.name < b2.name ? -1 : 1) : (a.file < b2.file ? -1 : 1)));
   659	    run.modifiedIntegrity = entries;
   660	  }
   661	  return run;
   662	}
   663	
   664	// Split a trustworthy --change run into the change verdict + the informative store report.
   665	// Fail-closed on failure signals: only a red BOUND to an out-of-scope projection scenario
   666	// is non-blocking; unattributed failures and FAILING true orphans block (no provenance).
   667	function applyChangeScope(run, meta) {
   668	  const full = run.verdict;                                  // whole-projection evaluation
   669	  const { pairs, ids, requirements } = meta;
   670	  const occCount = new Map();
   671	  ids.forEach((id) => { if (id) occCount.set(id, (occCount.get(id) || 0) + 1); });
   672	  const scopedIdSet = new Set();
   673	  const scopedUnidentified = [];
   674	  const allFiles = new Map();                                // id → Set(files), EVERY occurrence
   675	  pairs.forEach(([label, title, scoped], i) => {
   676	    const id = ids[i];
   677	    if (id) {
   678	      if (!allFiles.has(id)) allFiles.set(id, new Set());
   679	      allFiles.get(id).add(label);
   680	    }
   710	  };
   711	  run.changeScope = { requirements, scenarioIds: scopedIds };
   712	  run.verdict = { boundGreen, boundRed, unbound, orphan: failingOrphans,
   713	    unidentified: scopedUnidentified, unattributed, clean };
   714	  run.duplicates = scopedDup;
   715	  if (scopedIds.length === 0 && scopedUnidentified.length === 0)
   716	    run.vacuousNote = vacuousNoteFor(meta.deltaOps);
   717	}
   718	
   719	// machine-consumable shape for a verify run
   720	function verifyJson(run) {
   721	  const { verdict: v, results } = run;
   722	  const clean = v.clean && run.duplicates.length === 0 && run.errors.length === 0;
   723	  const json = projectionless(run, clean);
   724	  if (run.projection) json.projection = run.projection;   // ONLY --change runs carry this field
   725	  if (run.storeReport) {                                  // ONLY non-ERROR --change runs (absent otherwise)
   726	    json.storeReport = { ...run.storeReport,
   727	      unidentified: run.storeReport.unidentified.map(([file, title]) => ({ file, title })) };
   728	    json.changeScope = run.changeScope;
   729	    json.modifiedIntegrity = run.modifiedIntegrity;
   730	  }
   731	  return json;
   732	}
   733	
   734	function projectionless(run, clean) {
   735	  const { verdict: v, results } = run;
   736	  return {
   737	    clean,
   738	    result: run.errors.length ? 'ERROR' : (clean ? 'GREEN' : 'GAPS'),
   739	    errors: run.errors,
   740	    specFiles: run.fileCount,
   741	    exec: run.exec,
   742	    duplicates: run.duplicates,
   743	    boundGreen: v.boundGreen.map((id) => ({ id, ...results.get(id) })),
   744	    boundRed: v.boundRed.map((id) => ({ id, ...results.get(id) })),
   745	    unbound: v.unbound,
   746	    orphan: v.orphan.map((id) => ({ id, ...results.get(id) })),
   747	    unidentified: v.unidentified.map(([file, title]) => ({ file, title })),
   748	    unattributedFailures: { count: (v.unattributed || []).length, lines: v.unattributed || [] },
   749	    stderr: run.stderr || '',
   750	  };
   751	}
   752	
   753	// Fallback test command from apriori/process-config.md's `test-cmd` row (written by init --test-cmd)
   754	function configTestCmd(cwd) {
   755	  const { value, problem } = require('./config').getConfig(cwd, 'test-cmd');
   756	  if (problem) return { error: problem };
   757	  return value;
   758	}
   759	
   760	const USAGE = 'usage: apriori verify --specs <dir...> --test-cmd "<cmd>" [--id-pattern <re>] [--cwd <dir>] [--json]\n   or: apriori verify --change <name> --test-cmd "<cmd>" [--id-pattern <re>] [--cwd <dir>] [--json]\n(--test-cmd may be omitted when apriori/process-config.md has a test-cmd row;\n --id-pattern may be omitted when apriori/process-config.md has an id-pattern row)';
   761	
   762	function cli(argv) {
   763	  return withStrict(argv, { sub: 'verify', usage: USAGE, positionals: 0,
   764	    flags: { '--specs': 'multi', '--change': 'value', '--test-cmd': 'value', '--id-pattern': 'value', '--cwd': 'value', '--json': 'flag' } }, (f) => {
   765	    const a = { specs: f['--specs'] || [], change: f['--change'] || null, testCmd: f['--test-cmd'] || null,
   766	      idPattern: ('--id-pattern' in f) ? f['--id-pattern'] : null,   // PRESENCE, not truthiness (SR-52 empty flag)
   767	      cwd: f['--cwd'] || '.', json: !!f['--json'] };
   768	    if (a.change && a.specs.length) {
   769	      console.error('verify: --specs cannot be combined with --change — the projection defines the spec set');
   770	      return 2;
   771	    }
   772	    if (!a.testCmd) {
   773	      const c = configTestCmd(a.cwd);                    // init --test-cmd persists it there
   774	      if (c && c.error) { console.error(`verify: ${c.error}`); return 2; }
   775	      a.testCmd = c;
   776	    }
   777	    if ((!a.specs.length && !a.change) || !a.testCmd) { console.error(USAGE); return 2; }
   778	    const run = verify(a);
   779	    if (run.projection)
   780	      for (const sfx of run.projection.unstampedMutations || []) console.error('warning: ' + am.unstampedWarning(sfx));
   781	    if (run.errors.length && !a.json)
   782	      for (const e of run.errors) console.error('error: ' + e);
   783	    console.log(a.json ? JSON.stringify(verifyJson(run), null, 2)
   784	                       : formatReport(run.verdict, run.results, run.fileCount, { duplicates: run.duplicates, errors: run.errors, stderr: run.stderr, storeReport: run.storeReport, vacuousNote: run.vacuousNote, modifiedIntegrity: run.modifiedIntegrity }));
   785	    if (run.errors.length) return 2;
   786	    return run.verdict.clean && run.duplicates.length === 0 ? 0 : 1;
   787	  });
   788	}
   789	
   790	module.exports = { leadId, stripFences, collectScenarios, collectScenariosFromTexts, parseTap, looksLikeTap,
   791	  zeroTapParsed, runTestCommand, evaluate, infraErrors, formatReport, verifyJson, verify,
   792	  configTestCmd, cli, DEFAULT_ID, makeIdMatcher, _setChildRunner, matcherFailureMsg, CHILD_SPAWN_OPTS };
     1	#!/usr/bin/env node
     2	'use strict';
     3	/* apriori CLI — single self-contained entry, zero dependencies. Dispatches subcommands. */
     4	
     5	const sub = process.argv[2];
     6	const rest = process.argv.slice(3);
     7	
     8	const USAGE = `apriori <command>
     9	
    10	  new       scaffold a change dir + flow-state skeleton (bare kebab-case name)
    11	  status    show where a change is: step, next-action, open ledger items (--json)
    12	  verify    bind spec scenarios to test runs (STEP5 gate) (--json)
    13	  archive   merge a change's delta specs into the living store (STEP6)
    14	  stamp     print the CAS base-stamp line for a store file (delta authoring)
    15	  gate      aggregate the mechanical gate checks for one change (CI-friendly)
    16	  doctor    diagnose the project↔apriori seam (onboarding health check)
    17	  check     structural consistency checks (CI / pre-commit)
    18	  init      scaffold the workflow + per-tool pointers
    19	  update    refresh tool-owned files (runbook copy, command pointers) after a CLI upgrade
    20	
    21	Run 'apriori <command>' with no args for that command's usage.`;
    22	
    23	async function main() {
    24	  switch (sub) {
    25	    case 'new':     return require('../lib/new').cli(rest);
    26	    case 'status':  return require('../lib/status').cli(rest);
    27	    case 'verify':  return require('../lib/spec-runner').cli(rest);
    28	    case 'archive': return require('../lib/archive-merge').cli(rest, {
    29	      // the bin seam: archive-merge never requires spec-runner; the integrity report's
    30	      // terminable id matcher (config > default) is composed here and injected lazily
    31	      idMatcherFactory: (cwd) => {
    32	        const { resolveIdPattern } = require('../lib/config');
    33	        const r = resolveIdPattern(cwd, null);
    34	        if (r.error) return { error: r.error };
    35	        return require('../lib/spec-runner').makeIdMatcher(r);
    36	      },
    37	    });
    38	    case 'stamp':   return require('../lib/archive-merge').stampCli(rest);
    39	    case 'gate':    return require('../lib/gate').cli(rest);
    40	    case 'doctor':  return require('../lib/doctor').cli(rest);
    41	    case 'check':   return require('../lib/check').cli(rest);
    42	    case 'init':    return require('../lib/init').cli(rest);
    43	    case 'update':  return require('../lib/update').cli(rest);
    44	    case '-v':
    45	    case '--version': console.log(require('../package.json').version); return 0;
    46	    case undefined:
    47	    case '-h':
    48	    case '--help':  console.log(USAGE); return 0;
    49	    default:        console.error(`unknown command: ${sub}\n\n${USAGE}`); return 2;
    50	  }
    51	}
    52	
    53	main().then(
    54	  (code) => process.exit(code),
    55	  (err) => { console.error(`apriori: ${err && err.message ? err.message : err}`); process.exit(1); }
    56	);

   608	function archiveChange(o) {
   609	  const out = [], err = [];
   610	  const ops = o.ops || { writeFileSync: fs.writeFileSync.bind(fs), renameSync: fs.renameSync.bind(fs), rmSync: fs.rmSync.bind(fs) };
   611	  const cwd = o.cwd || process.cwd();
   612	  const changesDir = path.resolve(cwd, o.changesDir || path.join('apriori', 'changes'));
   613	  const storeRoot = path.resolve(cwd, 'apriori', 'specs');
   614	
   615	  // --- phase 1: preflight (no writes) ---
   616	  const d = discoverDeltas(changesDir, o.change);
   617	  if (d.errors.length) { err.push(...d.errors.map((e) => `archive: ${e}`)); return { code: 2, out, err }; }
   618	  const p = buildProjection(storeRoot, d.files, o.change);
   619	  if (p.validation.length) { err.push(...p.validation.map((e) => `archive: ${e}`)); return { code: 2, out, err }; }
   620	  // per-module report for whatever merged cleanly (dry-run info comes first, then failures)
   621	  for (const suffix of p.modules) {
   622	    const r = p.perModule.get(suffix);
   623	    if (!r || r.conflicts.length) continue;
   624	    out.push(`--- ${suffix}`);
   625	    for (const [label, key] of CATEGORY_LABELS) if (r[key].length) out.push(`${label}: ${r[key].join(', ')}`);
   626	  }
   627	  const casDenials = [];
   628	  if (p.unstampedMutations.length) {
   629	    const w = casWaiver(cwd, o.noCas);
   630	    if (w.problem) {
   631	      casDenials.push(`${w.problem} — a broken config never equals a waiver (denied)`);
   632	    } else if (!w.waiver) {
   633	      for (const sfx of p.unstampedMutations)
   634	        casDenials.push(`${unstampedWarning(sfx)} — denied by default; waive with --no-cas or a | cas | optional | config row`);
   635	    } else {
   636	      for (const sfx of p.unstampedMutations) out.push(`warning: ${unstampedWarning(sfx)}`);
   637	      out.push(`note: cas waived by ${w.waiver}`);
   638	    }
   639	  }
   640	  for (const n of p.notes) out.push(`note: ${n}`);
   641	  // CAS denial joins the preflight failure set — every diagnosis stays visible in one report
   642	  const preflightFailures = [...casDenials, ...p.hygiene, ...p.casMismatches, ...p.conflicts];
   643	  if (preflightFailures.length) {
   644	    for (const f of preflightFailures) err.push(`archive: ${f}`);
   645	    out.push('\nRESULT: FAILED PREFLIGHT — nothing written');
   646	    return { code: 1, out, err };
   647	  }
   648	  const repaired = new Set(p.repaired);                     // already applied — no writes for these
   649	  const jobs = d.files.filter(({ suffix }) => !repaired.has(suffix)).map(({ suffix }) => {
   650	    const store = path.join(storeRoot, ...suffix.split('/'));
   651	    return { suffix, store, tmp: store + '.tmp-archive', text: p.texts.get(suffix) };
   652	  }).sort((a, b) => (a.store < b.store ? -1 : 1));
   653	  if (o.write) {
   654	    for (const j of jobs) if (fs.existsSync(j.tmp)) {
   655	      err.push(`archive: temp file already exists: ${j.tmp} — another run in flight or a manual-recovery artifact; not touching it`);
   656	      out.push('\nRESULT: FAILED PREFLIGHT — nothing written');
   657	      return { code: 1, out, err };
   658	    }
   659	    if (o.changesDirExplicit) {
   660	      // destination containment is checked before any write, not at move time
   661	      const probe = path.join(changesDir, 'archive', 'probe');
   662	      if (!containsReal(changesDir, probe)) {
   663	        err.push(`archive: archive destination escapes ${changesDir} (symlinked archive/?)`);
   664	        return { code: 2, out, err };
   665	      }
   666	    }
   667	  }
   668	  // the modified-block integrity section: AFTER every preflight guard (incl. the --write
   669	  // temp/containment checks above), BEFORE any write-result line — informative only
   670	  pushIntegritySection(out, err, p.modifiedBlocks, o.idMatcherFactory, cwd);
   671	  if (!o.write) {
   672	    out.push(`\nRESULT: MERGED (dry-run; ${jobs.length} module(s)) — pass --write to apply`);
   673	    return { code: 0, out, err };
   674	  }
   675	
   676	  // --- phase 2: stage (this run's temps only — preflight proved none pre-existed) ---
   677	  const staged = [];
   678	  try {
   679	    for (const j of jobs) {
   680	      fs.mkdirSync(path.dirname(j.store), { recursive: true });
   681	      ops.writeFileSync(j.tmp, j.text);
   682	      staged.push(j.tmp);
   683	    }
   684	  } catch (e) {
   685	    for (const t of staged) { try { ops.rmSync(t, { force: true }); } catch { /* best effort */ } }
   686	    err.push(`archive: staging failed (${e.message}) — stores untouched, this run's temp files removed`);
   687	    return { code: 1, out, err };
   688	  }
   689	
   690	  // --- phase 3: commit (per-file atomic renames, sorted order; no rollback on mid-commit failure) ---
   691	  const committed = [];
   692	  for (const j of jobs) {
   693	    try { ops.renameSync(j.tmp, j.store); committed.push(j); }
   694	    catch (e) {
   695	      err.push(`archive: COMMIT FAILED at ${j.suffix} (${e.message})`);
   696	      err.push(`  committed:     ${committed.map((c) => c.suffix).join(', ') || '(none)'}`);
   697	      const remaining = jobs.filter((x) => !committed.includes(x));
   698	      err.push(`  NOT committed: ${remaining.map((x) => x.suffix).join(', ')}`);
   699	      err.push(`  temp files remaining for manual completion: ${remaining.map((x) => x.tmp).join(', ')}`);
   700	      return { code: 1, out, err };
   701	    }
   702	  }
   703	
   704	  // --- phase 4: move (only with explicit --changes-dir) ---
   705	  let moved = null;
   706	  if (o.changesDirExplicit) {
   707	    try { moved = archiveChangeDir(changesDir, o.change, new Date(), ops); }
   708	    catch (e) { err.push(`archive: stores committed but the change-dir move failed: ${e.message} — rerun to complete`); return { code: 1, out, err }; }
   709	    if (moved === null) { err.push('archive: stores committed but the change dir vanished before the move'); return { code: 1, out, err }; }
   710	  }
   711	  out.push(`\nRESULT: MERGED — ${jobs.length} module store(s) rewritten${moved ? `; change archived → ${moved}` : ''}`);
   712	  return { code: 0, out, err };
   713	}

   717	function stampCli(argv) {
   718	  return withStrict(argv, { sub: 'stamp', usage: STAMP_USAGE, positionals: 1, flags: {} }, (f, pos) => {
   719	    const p = pos[0];
   720	    if (!fs.existsSync(p)) { console.log('<!-- apriori-base: new -->'); return 0; }
   721	    if (fs.statSync(p).isDirectory()) { console.error(`stamp: ${p} is a directory, not a store file`); return 2; }
   722	    let text;
   723	    try { text = fs.readFileSync(p, 'utf8'); }
   724	    catch (e) { console.error(`stamp: cannot read ${p}: ${e.message}`); return 2; }
   725	    console.log(`<!-- apriori-base: ${fingerprint(text)} -->`);
   726	    return 0;
   727	  });
   728	}

   105	function scanTitles(text, label, pairs, preClean) {
   106	  const clean = preClean ? text : am.stripDeprecatedBlocks(stripFences(text));
   107	  let m;
   108	  SCENARIO_RE.lastIndex = 0;
   109	  while ((m = SCENARIO_RE.exec(clean)) !== null) pairs.push([label, m[1]]);
   110	}

   112	// One batch over tagged pairs ([label,title,scoped]) plus sibling titles (appended to the
   113	// SAME batch — never a third matcher call) → { acc, ids, siblingIds } or { failure }.
   114	function makeThenBind(pairs, matcher, siblingTitles = [], integrityTitles = []) {
   115	  const titles = [...pairs.map((p) => p[1]), ...siblingTitles, ...integrityTitles];
   116	  const res = matcher.batch(titles);
   117	  if (res.failure) return { failure: res.failure };
   118	  const acc = { byId: new Map(), idFiles: new Map(), unidentified: [] };
   119	  pairs.forEach(([label, title], i) => {
   120	    const id = res.ids[i];
   121	    if (id) {
   122	      if (!acc.byId.has(id)) { acc.byId.set(id, []); acc.idFiles.set(id, []); }
   123	      acc.byId.get(id).push(title);
   124	      acc.idFiles.get(id).push(label);
   125	    } else acc.unidentified.push([label, title]);
   126	  });
   127	  const sibSlice = res.ids.slice(pairs.length, pairs.length + siblingTitles.length);
   128	  const siblingIds = new Set(sibSlice.filter((x) => x !== null));
   129	  const integrityIds = res.ids.slice(pairs.length + siblingTitles.length);
   130	  return { acc, ids: res.ids.slice(0, pairs.length), siblingIds, integrityIds };
   131	}

   133	// Scenario titles a SIBLING active change genuinely declares — an EXEMPTION source, so it
   134	// is strict: each sibling delta must parse cleanly (parseDeltaStrict, zero problems, >0 ops)
   135	// and only scenarios inside its ADDED/MODIFIED block bodies count (a rename-only sibling's
   136	// scenarios already live in this change's projection and bind as out-of-scope reds).
   137	// Anomalies — malformed deltas, symlinks, escaping paths, unreadable files — grant NOTHING:
   138	// skipping is the fail-closed direction here (fewer exemptions = a stricter verdict); they
   139	// are never an ERROR, or a broken sibling could block every other change's gate.
   140	// fault-tolerant .md walk for the sibling scan ONLY: every readdir/lstat failure at any
   410	  for (const p of run.tapProblems || []) errs.push(`TAP protocol: ${p}`);
   411	  if (run.noTap)
   412	    errs.push('test command produced output but ZERO TAP results were parsed on stdout — the command is probably not emitting TAP (node test runner: add --test-reporter=tap; TAP on stderr: append 2>&1)');
   413	  // the TAP plan is a checked promise — defensive defaults keep legacy run shapes
   414	  // (direct callers of this exported helper) valid; only verify-created runs carry the fields
   415	  const plans = run.plans || [], points = run.points || 0, dupNumbers = run.dupNumbers || [];
   416	  if (plans.length > 1)
   417	    errs.push(`multiple TAP plans in one run (${plans.map((n) => `1..${n}`).join(', ')}) — cannot attribute test points to plans; run one TAP stream per verify (split the test command)`);
   418	  else if (plans.length === 1 && plans[0] !== points)
   419	    errs.push(`TAP plan declares ${plans[0]} test point(s) but ${points} were parsed — output truncated or garbled; refusing to trust this run`);
   420	  else if (plans.length === 1 && dupNumbers.length)
   421	    errs.push(`duplicate TAP test-point number(s): ${dupNumbers.join(', ')}`);
   422	  if (run.exec.status !== null && run.exec.status !== 0 && run.failCount === 0)
   423	    errs.push(`test command exited with status ${run.exec.status} but no parsed TAP failure explains it — the TAP picture is incomplete or truncated; refusing to trust this run`);
   424	  return errs;
   425	}

   427	function formatReport(v, results, fileCount, extras = {}) {
   428	  const lines = [];
   429	  const specCount = v.boundGreen.length + v.boundRed.length + v.unbound.length;
   430	  const tagged = [...results.values()].reduce((s, r) => s + r.pass + r.fail, 0);
   431	  const skipped = [...results.values()].reduce((s, r) => s + (r.skip || 0), 0);
   432	  lines.push(`specs: ${fileCount} file(s), ${specCount} identified scenario(s)`);
   433	  lines.push(`tests: ${tagged} tagged result(s)${skipped ? `, ${skipped} skipped (not counted)` : ''}\n`);
   434	  if (extras.vacuousNote) lines.push(`– ${extras.vacuousNote}`);
   435	  const groups = [['BOUND-GREEN', v.boundGreen], ['BOUND-RED', v.boundRed],
   436	                 ['UNBOUND (scenario, no test)', v.unbound], ['ORPHAN (test, no scenario)', v.orphan]];
   437	  for (const [label, items] of groups) {
   438	    if (!items.length) continue;
   439	    lines.push(`${label === 'BOUND-GREEN' ? '✓' : '✗'} ${label}: ${items.length}`);
   440	    for (const id of items) {
   441	      const r = results.get(id);
   442	      lines.push(`    ${id}${r ? `  ${r.pass}p/${r.fail}f${r.skip ? `/${r.skip}s` : ''}` : ''}`);
   443	    }
   444	  }
   445	  if (v.unattributed && v.unattributed.length) {
   446	    lines.push(`✗ UNATTRIBUTED FAILURES (not ok without a scenario ID): ${v.unattributed.length}`);
   447	    for (const raw of v.unattributed.slice(0, 20))
   448	      lines.push(`    ${raw.length > 120 ? raw.slice(0, 119) + '…' : raw}`);
   449	    if (v.unattributed.length > 20) lines.push(`    … and ${v.unattributed.length - 20} more`);
   450	  }
   451	  if (v.unidentified.length) {
   452	    lines.push(`✗ UNIDENTIFIED (scenario, no ID — unbindable): ${v.unidentified.length}`);
   453	    for (const [f, t] of v.unidentified) lines.push(`    ${path.basename(f)}: ${t.slice(0, 60)}`);
   454	  }
   455	  if (extras.duplicates && extras.duplicates.length) {
   456	    lines.push(`✗ DUPLICATE scenario IDs (ambiguous binding): ${extras.duplicates.length}`);
   457	    for (const d of extras.duplicates) lines.push(`    ${d.id}: ${d.files.map((f) => path.basename(f)).join(', ')}`);
   458	  }
   459	  if (extras.stderr && extras.stderr.trim()) {
   460	    const sl = extras.stderr.replace(/\r\n/g, '\n').replace(/\r/g, '\n').split('\n').filter((l) => l.length);
   461	    lines.push(`— STDERR DIAGNOSTICS (not TAP): ${sl.length} line(s)`);
   462	    for (const raw of sl.slice(0, 20)) lines.push(`    ${raw.length > 120 ? raw.slice(0, 119) + '…' : raw}`);
   463	    if (sl.length > 20) lines.push(`    … and ${sl.length - 20} more`);
   464	  }
   465	  if (extras.modifiedIntegrity) {
   466	    const seg = am.formatIntegrityHuman(extras.modifiedIntegrity);
   467	    if (seg) lines.push('\n' + seg);
   468	  }
   469	  if (extras.storeReport) {
   470	    // a STABLE six-class view: every class prints its count, non-empty classes list items
   471	    const sr2 = extras.storeReport;
   472	    lines.push('\n— STORE REPORT (informative; whole projection, same TAP snapshot) —');
   473	    lines.push(`    bound-green: ${sr2.boundGreen}`);
   474	    for (const [label, arr] of [['bound-red', sr2.boundRed], ['unbound', sr2.unbound], ['orphan', sr2.orphan]])
   475	      lines.push(`    ${label}: ${arr.length}${arr.length ? `  (${arr.join(', ')})` : ''}`);
   476	    lines.push(`    unidentified: ${sr2.unidentified.length}`);
   477	    for (const [f, t] of sr2.unidentified) lines.push(`        ${path.basename(f)}: ${t.slice(0, 60)}`);
   478	    lines.push(`    unattributed failures: ${sr2.unattributedFailures.count}`);
   586	// Full verify run. opts: { specs:[], change, testCmd, idPattern, cwd }
   587	function verify(opts) {
   588	  const cwd = opts.cwd || '.';
   589	  // pattern resolution FIRST — before any spec content is read or any test command spawned
   590	  const resolved = resolveIdPattern(cwd, opts.idPattern === undefined ? null : opts.idPattern);
   591	  if (resolved.error)
   592	    return errorsRun([resolved.error], opts.change ? enumerateProjection(opts.change, cwd) : null);
   593	  const idRe = new RegExp(resolved.source);
   594	  const matcher = makeIdMatcher(resolved);
   595	  let collected, projection = null, changeMeta = null;
   596	  if (opts.change) {
   597	    const b = buildChangeProjection(opts.change, cwd);
   598	    projection = b.projection;
   599	    if (!b.texts) return errorsRun(b.errors, projection);
   600	    // one pass over the projection: every occurrence collected once, tagged with its
   601	    // change-scope provenance; ONE matcher batch serves both views (SR-63).
   602	    // Sibling active changes' delta scenario titles join the SAME batch — a failing
   603	    // orphan whose ID a sibling declares is provably that change's business (GT-26).
   604	    const { pairs, requirements } = collectChangePairs(b.texts, b.deltaOps);
   605	    const siblingTitles = collectSiblingTitles(cwd, opts.change);
   606	    // OLD-block scenario titles ride the SAME batch (integrity report input; bind nothing)
   607	    const integrityTitles = [];
   608	    for (const list of (b.modifiedBlocks || new Map()).values())
   609	      for (const { oldBlock } of list) scanTitles(oldBlock, '(integrity)', integrityTitles);
   610	    const integrityTitleTexts = integrityTitles.map(([, t]) => t);
   611	    const batch = makeThenBind(pairs, matcher, siblingTitles, integrityTitleTexts);
   612	    if (batch.failure) return errorsRun([matcherFailureMsg(batch.failure)], projection);
   613	    collected = { byId: batch.acc.byId, unidentified: batch.acc.unidentified,
   614	      files: [...b.texts.keys()], missingTargets: [], duplicates: dupsOf(batch.acc) };
   615	    const titleId = new Map();
   616	    pairs.forEach(([, t], i) => { if (!titleId.has(t)) titleId.set(t, batch.ids[i]); });
   617	    integrityTitleTexts.forEach((t, i) => { if (!titleId.has(t)) titleId.set(t, batch.integrityIds[i]); });
   618	    changeMeta = { pairs, ids: batch.ids, requirements, deltaOps: b.deltaOps, siblingIds: batch.siblingIds,
   619	      modifiedBlocks: b.modifiedBlocks, titleId };
   620	  } else {
   621	    collected = collectScenarios(opts.specs, idRe, matcher);
   622	    if (collected.failure) return errorsRun([matcherFailureMsg(collected.failure)], projection);
   623	  }
   624	  const { byId, unidentified, files, missingTargets, duplicates } = collected;
   625	  const exec = runTestCommand(opts.testCmd, cwd);
   626	  const parsed = parseTap(exec.out, idRe, matcher);
   627	  if (parsed.matchFailure) {
   628	    const run = errorsRun([matcherFailureMsg(parsed.matchFailure)], projection);
   629	    run.exec = { status: exec.status, signal: exec.signal, error: exec.error };
   630	    run.stderr = exec.stderr || '';
   631	    return run;
   632	  }
   633	  const { results, untagged, untaggedFails, unattributedFailures, bailout, plans, points, dupNumbers, tapProblems } = parsed;
   634	  const verdict = evaluate(byId, unidentified, results, unattributedFailures);
   635	  const failCount = [...results.values()].reduce((s, r) => s + r.fail, 0) + untaggedFails;
   636	  const run = {
   637	    verdict, results, fileCount: files.length, failCount,
   638	    scenarioCount: byId.size + unidentified.length,
   639	    missingTargets, duplicates, bailout, plans, points, dupNumbers,
   640	    noTap: zeroTapParsed(exec.out, results, untagged, unattributedFailures)
   641	      || (!exec.out.trim() && looksLikeTap(exec.stderr)),  // TAP went to stderr — same zero-TAP cure (2>&1)
   642	    tapProblems,
   643	    stderr: exec.stderr || '',
   644	    exec: { status: exec.status, signal: exec.signal, error: exec.error },
   645	  };
   646	  if (projection) run.projection = projection;
   647	  run.errors = infraErrors(run);
   648	  // change-scoped verdict split (SR-56..62): only on trustworthy --change runs; every
   649	  // ERROR class leaves the run without storeReport/changeScope (absent, never null)
   650	  if (changeMeta && run.errors.length === 0) {
   651	    applyChangeScope(run, changeMeta);
   652	    // the modified-block integrity report (informative; ALWAYS present on GREEN/GAPS)
   653	    const idOf = (title) => { const v = changeMeta.titleId.get(title); return v === undefined ? null : v; };
   654	    const entries = [];
   655	    for (const [file, list] of (changeMeta.modifiedBlocks || new Map()))
   656	      for (const { name, oldBlock, newBlock } of list)
   657	        entries.push({ file, name, ...am.compareModifiedBlock(oldBlock, newBlock, idOf) });
   658	    entries.sort((a, b2) => (a.file === b2.file ? (a.name < b2.name ? -1 : 1) : (a.file < b2.file ? -1 : 1)));
   659	    run.modifiedIntegrity = entries;
   660	  }
   661	  return run;
   662	}

   181	// total number of operations a parsed delta carries (0 = nothing recognized/parseable)
   182	function deltaOpCount(delta) {
   183	  return delta.ADDED.size + delta.MODIFIED.size + delta.REMOVED.size + delta.RENAMED.length;
   184	}

   186	// mutation ops can silently clobber concurrent edits; ADDED-only deltas conflict or no-op instead
   187	function mutationOpCount(delta) {
   188	  return delta.MODIFIED.size + delta.REMOVED.size + delta.RENAMED.length;

   191	// the one warning text every surface shares (WARN grade this minor; 4.0 makes stamps mandatory)
   192	// 4.0: unstamped mutation deltas are DENIED by default; --no-cas or the config row waives visibly
   193	// waiver source, or {problem} when the config itself is broken (deny + name it) — the
   194	// explicit flag stays supreme even over a broken config (the flag is a human's will)
   195	function casWaiver(cwd, noCas) {
   196	  if (noCas) return { waiver: '--no-cas' };
   197	  const problem = configCasProblem(cwd);
   198	  if (problem) return { problem };
   199	  if (configCas(cwd) === 'optional') return { waiver: 'process-config cas: optional' };
   200	  return {};
   201	}

   205	}
